contig-100_2580_1	580331.Thit_1395	3.89e-13	75.1	COG0507@1|root,COG0507@2|Bacteria,1TQWH@1239|Firmicutes,24A8I@186801|Clostridia,42HVJ@68295|Thermoanaerobacterales	186801|Clostridia	L	A helicase nuclease that prepares dsDNA breaks (DSB) for recombinational DNA repair. Binds to DSBs and unwinds DNA via a highly rapid and processive ATP-dependent bidirectional helicase activity. Unwinds dsDNA until it encounters a Chi (crossover hotspot instigator) sequence from the 3' direction. Cuts ssDNA a few nucleotides 3' to the Chi site. The properties and activities of the enzyme are changed at Chi.
